exec
"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "@'
import json
p='translations/audit/queue/101_flags_en.json'
data=json.load(open(p,encoding='utf-8'))
print('records',len(data),'unique',len(set(x['n'] for x in data)))
